- **i18n**: `apps/web` and `apps/native` are internationalized with [Lingui](https://lingui.dev) v6. This document covers the repo-specific setup; generic Lingui API usage follows the official docs (macros from `@lingui/react/macro` and `@lingui/core/macro`). ([Insik-Han/han-monorepo-template](https://github.com/Insik-Han/han-monorepo-template/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/i18n.md) · ★17 · 2 copies)
- **i18n**: Internationalization expert for Arabic/English, RTL/LTR, dictionary system, and translation helpers ([databayt/hogwarts](https://github.com/databayt/hogwarts/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/i18n.md) · ★16)
- **i18n**: - Translation keys follow pattern: `pages.{pageName}.{keyName}` - Example: `t("pages.index.exportRecipeBook")` - Translation files must be updated in locales: `en`, `pt` - Files located in: `public/locales/{locale}/translation.json` - Use `{{variable}}` syntax for variable interpolation (e.g., `{{count}}`) ([jlucaspains/sharp-cooking-web](https://github.com/jlucaspains/sharp-cooking-web/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/i18n.md) · ★13)
- **i18n Accessibility**: Internationalization and RTL accessibility specialist. Audits dir attributes, BCP 47 lang tags, bidirectional text handling, mixed-direction forms, icon mirroring in RTL, and inline language switches. Ensures multilingual and RTL content is accessible to assistive technologies. ([Community-Access/accessibility-agents](https://github.com/Community-Access/accessibility-agents/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/i18n-accessibility.agent.md))
- **i18n Agent**: Agent: Manage translations and internationalization for simpler-grants-gov frontend. Invoke manually when adding or modifying user-facing text. ([btabaska/simpler-grants-documentation-automation](https://github.com/btabaska/simpler-grants-documentation-automation/blob/HEAD/.cursor/agents/i18n.md) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **i18n Completeness Checker**: Specialist reviewer subagent. Invoked BY OTHER AGENTS (i18n, user-guide-updater, codemod, new-endpoint) as a quality gate. Verifies that every user-facing string is wrapped in a translation function, present in every locale bundle, and free of hardcoded English fallbacks. Not invoked directly by users. ([btabaska/simpler-grants-documentation-automation](https://github.com/btabaska/simpler-grants-documentation-automation/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/i18n-completeness-checker.md) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **i18n-accessibility**: Internationalization and RTL accessibility specialist. Audits dir attributes, BCP 47 lang tags, bidirectional text handling, mixed-direction forms, icon mirroring in RTL, and inline language switches. Ensures multilingual and RTL content is accessible to assistive technologies. - **i18n-agent**: Multilingual translation agent for KinderSpark Pro. Use when adding new UI text, adding a new language, finding missing translations, or syncing translations across web/iOS/Android. - **i18n-check**: Checks whether a change to frontend/src/locales/en.json or de.json left the two files out of sync (a key added/renamed/removed in one but not the other). Use proactively after editing either locale file, or after adding a new translation key reference (t("...")) in a component. ([maik-hasler/einsatzbereit](https://github.com/maik-hasler/einsatzbereit/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/i18n-check.md) · ★5)
- **i18n-checker**: i18n Checker — verifies locale key parity, detects hardcoded UI strings, and validates translation format consistency. Activated when i18n domain is detected (locale files, translation keys). ([berrzebb/quorum](https://github.com/berrzebb/quorum/blob/HEAD/platform/adapters/openai-compatible/agents/i18n-checker.md) · ★10 · 2 copies)
- **i18n-checker**: Checks translation completeness across all languages (NL, EN, DE). Use after adding new translation keys or creating new pages. ([OpenHospi/OpenHospi](https://github.com/OpenHospi/OpenHospi/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/i18n-checker.md) · ★2)
- **i18n-checker**: 하드코딩된 문자열 감지, 번역 키 누락, 미사용 번역 키를 정리하는 에이전트 ([MyoungSoo7/eln-convenient](https://github.com/MyoungSoo7/eln-convenient/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/i18n-checker.md) · ★1)
- **i18n-code**: 接收 i18n-validate 产出的 issues JSON 报告，对每条命中做语义判断（真问题 / 误报 / 需上下文），输出结构化 verdicts JSON。 不自行 grep 或扫描项目，输入由主线程通过 validator 提供。 当用户说"审核i18n"、"判断替换问题"、"review i18n issues"时触发。 ([Chyelong/i18n-vue-plugin](https://github.com/Chyelong/i18n-vue-plugin/blob/HEAD/agents/i18n-code.md))
- **i18n-consistency-reviewer**: 审查 zh/en/ja 三语 locale 的一致性——重点是编译器抓不到的多余 key、未翻译残留、以及绕过 useLanguage() 的硬编码 UI 文案 ([hyhmrright/StockAI](https://github.com/hyhmrright/StockAI/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/i18n-consistency-reviewer.md) · ★10)
- **i18n-editor**: Use this agent for ANY i18n/translation work - adding, updating, or removing translation keys in the `en` and `uk` locale JSON files (every key change lands in both). Trigger phrases include "add translation", "update translation", "add i18n key", "localization", "translate", "add to en.json", "locale files". The main model CANNOT read i18n files (blocked by hook to save tokens), so this agent MUST be used for all i18n operations. ([letehaha/budget-tracker](https://github.com/letehaha/budget-tracker/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/i18n-editor.md) · ★138)
- **i18n-engineer**: Refactors hardcoded user-facing strings into the project's own i18n mechanism and generates locale resources, in whatever stack the project declares. Phase 6, on demand. A repo-wide change, so it hands off to qa-engineer before approval. ([AZidan/archflow](https://github.com/AZidan/archflow/blob/HEAD/adapters/gemini/agents/i18n-engineer.md) · ★28 · 5 copies)
- **i18n-engineer**: Use this agent when adding internationalization (i18n) to a React, Vue 3, Svelte, or React Native app. Sets up locale management, implements RTL support, and handles translations. Specializes in next-intl, react-i18next, vue-i18n, svelte-i18n, and expo-localization. - **i18n-expert**: Use for any task involving multi-locale design, translation completeness, or locale-aware behaviour - `pokemonNameLocale`, `appLocale`, transliteration sources (rōmaji, pinyin), message catalogs, locale routing (`next-intl`), locale-aware sync, `&lt;lang&gt;` attribute placement, or adding a new locale. Trigger phrases include "extract these strings", "internationalize this component", "move hardcoded text to the catalog", "wrap these strings in t()", "add i18n to this file", "these strings aren't translatable". - **i18n-guardian**: Locale and translation work across all four i18n trees (packages/www translations, packages/cli locales, private/account/web locales, private/renet Go catalogs). Knows the single-source locale set, the naturalization workflow, every i18n gate including the 2026-08 additions (generalized cross-locale detection, German de-contamination with shrink-only baseline, renet locale-value quality tests), and the contamination classes that shipped undetected for months. Use for adding/changing user-facing strings, locale deltas, translation quality sweeps, or diagnosing an i18n gate failure. ([davidteren/intent-engineering](https://github.com/davidteren/intent-engineering/blob/HEAD/plugins/intent-engineering/agents/ie-experience-reviewer.md) · ★3)
